Fine.\n\nKernel design details:\n```\nconstexpr int TILE = 256;\n__global__ void nbody_step_kernel(double* __restrict__ pos, // size 3n, AoS\n                                  double* __restrict__ vel, // size 3*localN, local AoS\n                                  int n, int localStart, int localN)\n{\n    const int t = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = t < localN;\n    const int i = localStart + t;\n    __shared__ double s[3*TILE];\n    double ix=0, iy=0, iz=0;\n    if (active) { ix=pos[3*i]; iy=pos[3*i+1]; iz=pos[3*i+2]; }\n    double Fx=0,Fy=0,Fz=0;\n    for (int base=0; base<n; base+=TILE) {\n        const int lj = threadIdx.x;\n        const int gj = base + lj;\n        if (gj < n) { s[3*lj]=pos[3*gj]; s[3*lj+1]=...; } else { s[3*lj]=0;...}\n        __syncthreads();\n        const int cnt = min(TILE, n-base);\n        if (active) {\n            for (int j=0;j<cnt;++j) {\n                double dx=s[3*j]-ix; ...\n                double distSqr=dx*dx+dy*dy+dz*dz+SOFTENING;\n                double invDist=1.0/sqrt(distSqr);\n                double invDist3=invDist*invDist*invDist;\n                Fx+=dx*invDist3; ...\n            }\n        }\n        __syncthreads();\n    }\n    if (active) {\n        double vx = vel[3*t] + DT*Fx; // local index\n        ...\n        vel[3*t]=vx;...\n        pos[3*i]=ix+vx*DT; ...\n    }\n}\n```\nNote original: computes all forces using old positions for all i, then integrates all. Our kernel updates pos[i] as it goes. Does that affect other threads' force computation? Other threads read positions from shared memory loaded before any updates? No—the shared load happens per tile at each iteration from global pos, which may already be updated by threads in earlier tiles! Because thread updates pos at the very end after all tiles, so within kernel all force computations complete before updates (each thread updates only after its full loop). Updates happen at end of each thread; but different blocks may finish at different times and update pos while other blocks are still loading tiles for later iterations → data race! Because all blocks process the same full j range independently. A block that finishes early updates its local pos slice, while a slower block reading pos in later tiles sees updated positions. That breaks semantics.\n\nSolutions:\n- Separate kernels: compute forces (write d_vel += DT*F) then integrate kernel updating pos. Two kernels with implicit ordering on same stream. Then allgather. Explicit fma()\n// calls reproduce the contraction chosen by the host compiler for the\n// reference build; --fmad=false prevents any additional contraction.\n__global__ void computeForcesKernel(const                                   const int localN) {\n    const int t = blockIdx.x * blockDim.x + threadIdx.x;\n    const bool active = t < localN;\n    const int i = localStart + t;\n\n    double ix rep < reps; ++rep) {\n            const auto t0 = clock::now();\n            if (count > 0) {\n                gpuLaunchForces(gpu, allpos, n, localStart, count);\n                if (count < localN) {\n                    computeForcesCPURange(allpos, localpos, tmpvel, n, count, localN);\n                }\n                gpuDownloadForces(gpu, tmpvel, count);\n            } else {\n                computeForcesCPURange(allpos, localpos, tmpvel, n, 0, localN);\n            }\n            const auto t1 = clock::now();\n            best = std::min(best, std::chrono::duration<double, std::milli>(t1 - t0).count());\n        }\n        if (best < bestTime) {\n            bestTime = best;\n            bestCount = count;\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Restore the device velocities modified during calibration.\n    const size_t velBytes = 3 * sizeof(double) * static_cast<size_t>(localN);\n    cudaMemcpy(gpu.d_vel, localvel.data(), velBytes, cudaMemcpyHostToDevice);\n\n    return bestCount;\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    MPI_Init(&argc, &argv);\n\n    int rank = 0;\n    int size = 1;\n    MPI_Comm_rank(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &rank);\n    MPI_Comm_size(MPI_COMM_WORLD, &size);\n\n    int numBodies = 1024;\n    int numSteps = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    int exitCode = 0;\n\n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numBodies = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-s\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            numSteps = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            if (rank == 0) {\n                printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n                printUsage(argv[0]);\n            }\n            MPI_Finalize();\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n","exit":0,"truncated":false},"title":"cd /home/llmtest/evals/20261019-134042/nbody_deepseek-v4.1-flash-oc-high_hybrid_r2/nbody && grep -n \"DBG\\|computeForcesCPU(\\|gpuStep\" nbody.cpp;
